50: class Simulator:
51:     def __init__(self, config, root, code_commit, *, fault=None):
52:         self.config = config
53:         self.namespace = uuid.UUID(config["request_id"])
54:         self.root = root
55:         self.code_commit = code_commit
56:         self.fault = fault
57:         self.store = TransactionStore(
58:             root / "transactions", initial_state(money(config["initial_cash"]), config["assets"])
59:         )
60:         # Recover by deterministic re-execution against immutable committed prefixes.
61:         # Existing commands are compared, not applied a second time or recomputed from the final state.
62:         self.store.state = deepcopy(self.store.initial)
63:         self.logical_version = 0
64:         self.provider = SignalProvider(config["decisions"])
65:         self.queue = []
66:         self.seq = 0
67:         self.trace = []
68:         self.portfolios = []
69:         self.decisions = {}
70:         self.current_key = None
71:         self.session_ids = {s["session_id"]: i for i, s in enumerate(config["sessions"])}
72:         self._schedule()
73: 
74:     def uid(self, text):
75:         return str(uuid.uuid5(self.namespace, text))
76: 
77:     def enqueue(self, at, phase, kind, data):
78:         at = utc(at)
79:         if self.current_key is not None and (at, phase) < self.current_key[:2]:
80:             raise ContractError("EVENT_INSERTION_BEFORE_CURRENT_PHASE")
81:         self.seq += 1
82:         heapq.heappush(self.queue, (at, phase, self.seq, kind, data))
83: 
84:     def _schedule(self):
85:         for i, s in enumerate(self.config["sessions"]):
86:             self.enqueue(s["open"], 10, "SETTLEMENT", {"session": i})
87:             for kind in ("OPEN", "CLOSE"):
88:                 rows = [
89:                     r
90:                     for r in self.config["sidecar"]["rows"]
91:                     if r["session_id"] == s["session_id"] and r["kind"] == kind
92:                 ]
93:                 on_time = [
94:                     r
95:                     for r in rows
96:                     if utc(r["available_at"]) == utc(r["event_time"])
97:                     and utc(r["known_at"]) <= utc(r["event_time"])
98:                 ]
99:                 self.enqueue(
100:                     s[kind.lower()], 20, "MARKET", {"session": i, "kind": kind, "rows": on_time}
101:                 )
102:                 for r in rows:
103:                     if r not in on_time:
104:                         self.enqueue(
105:                             max(utc(r["available_at"]), utc(r["known_at"])), 20, "LATE_SLICE", r
106:                         )
107:             self.enqueue(s["close"], 65, "EXPIRY", {"session": i})
108:             self.enqueue(s["close"], 95, "BORROW", {"session": i})
109:             self.enqueue(s["close"], 100, "VALUATION", {"session": i})
110:             self.enqueue(s["open"], 100, "OPEN_VALUATION", {"session": i})
111:         for index, d in enumerate(self.config["decisions"]):
112:             at = load(d["portfolio_request_utf8"])["context"]["decision_time"]
113:             self.enqueue(at, 30, "SIGNAL", {"index": index, "decision": d})
114:         scheduled_actions = []
115:         priorities = {"SPLIT": 1, "DIVIDEND": 2, "DELIST": 4}
116:         end = utc(self.config["sessions"][-1]["close"])
117:         for a in self.config["actions"]:
118:             if utc(a["available_at"]) > utc(a["effective_at"]):
119:                 scheduled_actions.append((utc(a["available_at"]), 0, "LATE_ACTION", a))
120:             else:
121:                 scheduled_actions.append(
122:                     (utc(a["effective_at"]), priorities[a["kind"]], a["kind"], a)
123:                 )
124:                 if a["kind"] == "DIVIDEND":
125:                     scheduled_actions.append((utc(a["pay_at"]), 3, "DIVIDEND_PAY", a))
126:         for at, _, kind, a in sorted(
127:             scheduled_actions, key=lambda v: (v[0], v[1], v[3]["action_id"])
128:         ):
129:             if at <= end:
130:                 self.enqueue(at, 10, kind, a)
131:         for f in self.config["cashflows"]:
132:             self.enqueue(f["at"], 10, "CASHFLOW", f)
133:         for c in self.config["cancellations"]:
134:             received = utc(c["received_at"])
135:             # A control delivered during70 cannot back-insert65; acknowledgement next UTCus.
136:             during_match = c["received_phase"] > 65
137:             ack = received + timedelta(microseconds=1) if during_match else received
138:             self.enqueue(
139:                 ack,
140:                 65,
141:                 "CANCELLATION",
142:                 {**c, "ack_at": timestamp(ack), "deferred_during_match": during_match},
143:             )
144: 
145:     def _observe(self, kind, payload):
146:         self.trace.append(
147:             {
148:                 "dispatch_key": [timestamp(self.current_key[0]), *self.current_key[1:]],
149:                 "kind": kind,
150:                 "read_version": self.logical_version,
151:                 "payload": freeze(payload),
152:             }
153:         )
154: 
155:     def _commit(self, kind, payload, operation, *, key=None):
156:         expected_hash = digest(freeze(self.store.state))
157:         post = deepcopy(self.store.state)
158:         children = operation(post)
159:         assert_economic_identity(post)
160:         if post == self.store.state:
161:             self._observe(kind, {"unchanged": True, "children": children})
162:             return None
163: 
164:         def apply(s):
165:             if digest(freeze(s)) != expected_hash:
166:                 raise ContractError("STALE_TRANSACTION_PRESTATE")
167:             s.clear()
168:             s.update(deepcopy(post))
169:             return children
170: 
171:         event_key = key or self.uid(kind + ":" + str(self.current_key))
172:         dispatch = [timestamp(self.current_key[0]), *self.current_key[1:]]
173:         item = self.store.commit(event_key, payload, dispatch, apply, fault=self.fault)
174:         if (
175:             item["pre_hash"] != expected_hash
176:             or item["post_hash"] != digest(freeze(post))
177:             or item["children"] != [{"child_seq": i, **freeze(v)} for i, v in enumerate(children)]
178:             or item["commit_seq"] != self.logical_version + 1
179:         ):
180:             raise ContractError("RECOVERY_NONDETERMINISTIC_TRANSACTION")
181:         self.logical_version += 1
182:         self.store.state = thaw(item["post_state"])
183:         self._observe(kind, {"commit_seq": item["commit_seq"], "sha256": item["sha256"]})
184:         return item
185: 
186:     def run(self):
187:         while self.queue:
188:             at, phase, seq, kind, payload = heapq.heappop(self.queue)
189:             self.current_key = (at, phase, seq)
190:             self._dispatch(kind, payload)
191:         return self.store.state
192: 
193:     def _dispatch(self, kind, d):
194:         if kind == "SETTLEMENT":
195: 
196:             def apply(s):
197:                 s["session"] = d["session"]
198:                 settle(s, d["session"], self.uid("settle:" + str(d["session"])))
199:                 return [{"kind": "AccountUpdated", "local_stage": 90}]
200: 
201:             self._commit(kind, d, apply)
202:         elif kind == "MARKET":
203:             self._market(d)
204:         elif kind == "LATE_SLICE":
205:             self._observe(kind, d)
206:         elif kind == "RISK":
207:             self._risk_portfolio(d["index"])
208:         elif kind == "LATE_ACTION":
209:             raise ContractError("DATA_REVISION_REQUIRES_RERUN")
210:         elif kind == "SIGNAL":
211:             request = self.provider.release(self.current_key[0])
212:             self.decisions[d["index"]] = {"original": request, "configuration": d["decision"]}
213:             self._observe(
214:                 kind,
215:                 {
216:                     "request_sha256": request.content_hash,
217:                     "signal_identity": signal_identity(request),
218:                 },
219:             )
220:             self.enqueue(self.current_key[0], 40, "PORTFOLIO", {"index": d["index"]})
221:         elif kind == "PORTFOLIO":
222:             self._portfolio(d["index"])
223:         elif kind == "SUBMISSION":
224:             self._submit(d["index"])
225:         elif kind == "MATCH":
226:             self._match(d)
227:         elif kind in ("EXPIRY", "CANCELLATION"):
228:             self._cancel(kind, d)
229:         elif kind == "BORROW":
230:             self._borrow(d)
231:         elif kind == "VALUATION":
232:             self._valuation(d)
233:         elif kind == "OPEN_VALUATION":
234:             self._observe(kind, readonly_snapshot(self.store.state))
235:         elif kind == "CASHFLOW":
236:             self._flow(d)
237:         else:
238:             self._action(kind, d)
239: 
240:     def _market(self, d):
241:         def apply(s):
242:             for r in d["rows"]:
243:                 a = s["assets"][r["security_id"]]
244:                 if a["delisted"] and r["tradable"]:
245:                     raise ContractError("DELISTED_SECURITY_REACTIVATION")
246:                 if r["status"] == "DELISTED" and a["quantity"]:
247:                     raise ContractError("UNRESOLVED_DELISTING")
248:                 a.update(
249:                     tradable=r["tradable"],
250:                     in_universe=r["in_universe"],
251:                     short_permission=r["short_permission"],
252:                     borrow_limit=money(r["borrow_limit"]),
253:                     industry=r["industry"],
254:                     beta=money(r["beta"]),
255:                 )
256:                 if r["price"] is not None:
257:                     a.update(
258:                         mark=money(r["price"]), last_mark_session=d["session"], mark_ref=deepcopy(r)
259:                     )
260:                     record_valuation(s, r["security_id"], r["slice_id"] + ":valuation")
261:                 if d["kind"] == "OPEN":
262:                     history = visible_adv(self.config, r["security_id"], self.current_key[0])
263:                     s["capacity"][r["slice_id"]] = (
264:                         min(F(10), history["average"]) if history["passed"] else F(0)
265:                     )
266:             return [{"kind": "MarketConfirmed", "local_stage": 20}]
267: 
268:         self._commit("MARKET", d, apply)
269:         if account(self.store.state)["available"] < 0:
270:             raise ContractError("MARGIN_BREACH")
271:         if d["kind"] == "OPEN":
272:             rows = {r["security_id"]: r for r in d["rows"]}
273:             orders = sorted(
274:                 self.store.state["orders"].values(),
275:                 key=lambda o: (utc(o["submitted_at"]), o["order_id"]),
276:             )
277:             for o in orders:
278:                 if (
279:                     o["status"] in LIVE
280:                     and utc(o["eligible_at"]) <= self.current_key[0]
281:                     and o["security_id"] in rows
282:                 ):
283:                     self.enqueue(
284:                         self.current_key[0],
285:                         70,
286:                         "MATCH",
287:                         {"order_id": o["order_id"], "slice": rows[o["security_id"]]},
288:                     )
289: 
290:     def _portfolio(self, index):
291:         dec = self.decisions[index]
292:         budget_id = self.uid("decision:" + str(index))
293: 
294:         def apply(s):
295:             if any(o["status"] in LIVE for o in s["orders"].values()):
296:                 raise ContractError("REBALANCE_PENDING_ORDERS")
297:             s["budgets"][budget_id] = {
298:                 "total": money(self.config["fee_budget"]),
299:                 "paid": F(0),
300:                 "turnover_notional": F(0),
301:                 "decision_equity": account(s)["equity"],
302:             }
303:             s["current_budget"] = budget_id
304:             return [{"kind": "CommissionBudgetRegistered", "local_stage": 40}]
305: 
306:         self._commit("BUDGET", {"index": index}, apply)
307:         request, projection = adapt_portfolio(dec["original"], self.store.state)
308:         candidate = build_candidate(request)
309:         dec.update(request=request, budget_id=budget_id, projection=projection)
310:         self._observe(
311:             "PORTFOLIO_DECISION",
312:             {
313:                 "index": index,
314:                 "request_hash": request.content_hash,
315:                 "candidate": load(candidate.raw),
316:                 "projection": projection,
317:             },
318:         )
319:         self.enqueue(self.current_key[0], 50, "RISK", {"index": index})
320: 
321:     def _risk_portfolio(self, index):
322:         dec = self.decisions[index]
323:         request, projection = dec["request"], dec["projection"]
324:         result = construct_engineering(
325:             request, self.root / "portfolio-runs", self.code_commit
326:         ).read()
327:         verify_portfolio_result(request, result, self.root / "portfolio-runs")
328:         dec["result"] = result
329:         self.portfolios.append(
330:             {
331:                 "index": index,
332:                 "request_utf8": request.raw.decode("utf-8"),
333:                 "projection": freeze(projection),
334:                 "result": result,
335:             }
336:         )
337:         self._observe(
338:             "RISK",
339:             {
340:                 "index": index,
341:                 "status": result["status"],
342:                 "actual_P4_run_id": result["run_id"],
343:                 "projection": projection,
344:             },
345:         )
346:         if result["status"] == "SUCCEEDED":
347:             self.enqueue(self.current_key[0], 60, "SUBMISSION", {"index": index})
348: 
349:     def _submit(self, index):
350:         dec = self.decisions[index]
351:         cfg = dec["configuration"]
352:         verify_portfolio_result(dec["request"], dec["result"], self.root / "portfolio-runs")
353:         submitted = self.current_key[0]
354:         eligible = next(
355:             (i for i, v in enumerate(self.config["sessions"]) if utc(v["open"]) > submitted), None
356:         )
357:         if eligible is None:
358:             self._observe(
359:                 "SUBMISSION_REJECTED", {"reason": "NO_FUTURE_EXECUTION_SESSION", "index": index}
360:             )
361:             return
362:         expires = (
363:             eligible if cfg["time_in_force"] == "DAY" else self.session_ids[cfg["expires_session"]]
364:         )
365:         if expires < eligible:
366:             raise ContractError("EXPIRY_BEFORE_ELIGIBILITY")
367:         request_x = load(dec["request"].raw)
368: 
369:         def apply(s):
370:             orders = []
371:             for sid, delta in sorted(dec["result"]["target"]["deltas"].items()):
372:                 signed = serialized_number(delta)
373:                 if not signed:
374:                     continue
375:                 a = s["assets"][sid]
376:                 oid = self.uid(f"order:{index}:{sid}")
377:                 low, high = bounds(
378:                     a["mark"],
379:                     "BUY" if signed > 0 else "SELL",
380:                     cfg["order_type"],
381:                     money(cfg["limit_prices"][sid]) if cfg["order_type"] == "LIMIT" else None,
382:                 )
383:                 q = abs(signed)
384:                 short_increase = max(-(a["quantity"] + signed), 0) - max(-a["quantity"], 0)
385:                 reserve = q * high if signed > 0 else max(short_increase, 0) * high
386:                 o = {
387:                     "order_id": oid,
388:                     "security_id": sid,
389:                     "original_qty": q,
390:                     "filled_qty": F(0),
391:                     "cancelled_qty": F(0),
392:                     "expired_remaining": F(0),
393:                     "remaining": q,
394:                     "signed_remaining": signed,
395:                     "side": "BUY" if signed > 0 else "SELL",
396:                     "kind": cfg["order_type"],
397:                     "time_in_force": cfg["time_in_force"],
398:                     "submitted_at": timestamp(submitted),
399:                     "eligible_at": timestamp(self.config["sessions"][eligible]["open"]),
400:                     "expires_session": expires,
401:                     "low": low,
402:                     "high": high,
403:                     "lot": a["lot"],
404:                     "reserve_notional": reserve,
405:                     "budget_id": dec["budget_id"],
406:                     "constraints": request_x["constraints"],
407:                     "P4_request_utf8": dec["request"].raw.decode("utf-8"),
408:                     "P4_request_sha256": dec["request"].content_hash,
409:                     "P4_risk_sha256": dec["result"]["risk"]["content_sha256"],
410:                     "P4_target_sha256": dec["result"]["target"]["content_sha256"],
411:                     "decision_time": request_x["context"]["decision_time"],
412:                     "reference_price": a["mark"],
413:                     "submission_liquidity": visible_adv(self.config, sid, submitted),
414:                     "limit_price": money(cfg["limit_prices"][sid])
415:                     if cfg["order_type"] == "LIMIT"
416:                     else None,
417:                     "status": "CREATED",
418:                     "history": [{"status": "CREATED", "reason": None}],
419:                 }
420:                 if (signed / a["lot"]).denominator != 1 and signed != -a["quantity"]:
421:                     raise ContractError("DELTA_LOT_MISMATCH")
422:                 transition(o, "SUBMITTED")
423:                 s["orders"][oid] = o
424:                 orders.append(oid)
425:             c = account(s)
426:             new_n = sum((s["orders"][oid]["reserve_notional"] for oid in orders), F(0))
427:             new_f = sum(
428:                 (
429:                     fee_upper(s["orders"][oid]["remaining"], s["orders"][oid]["high"])
430:                     for oid in orders
431:                 ),
432:                 F(0),
433:             )
434:             liquidity_passed = all(
435:                 s["orders"][oid]["submission_liquidity"]["passed"] for oid in orders
436:             )
437:             accepted = (
438:                 liquidity_passed and c["F_unused"] >= new_f and c["available"] >= new_n + new_f
439:             )
440:             for oid in orders:
441:                 transition(
442:                     s["orders"][oid],
443:                     "ACCEPTED" if accepted else "REJECTED",
444:                     None
445:                     if accepted
446:                     else "ADV_HISTORY_OR_THRESHOLD_REJECTED"
447:                     if not liquidity_passed
448:                     else "ORDER_BASKET_BUDGET_OR_CASH_EXCEEDED",
449:                 )
450:                 if not accepted:
451:                     s["orders"][oid]["reserve_notional"] = F(0)
452:             return [
453:                 {
454:                     "kind": "OrdersAccepted" if accepted else "OrdersRejected",
455:                     "local_stage": 60,
456:                     "order_ids": orders,
457:                 }
458:             ]
459: 
460:         self._commit(
461:             "SUBMISSION", {"index": index, "P4_request_hash": dec["request"].content_hash}, apply
462:         )
463: 
464:     def _p4_post(self, post, before, order):
465:         x, maps, _, _ = prepare(PortfolioRequest(order["P4_request_utf8"].encode()))
466:         x = deepcopy(x)
467:         c = account(post)
468:         x["inputs"]["account"]["data"].update(
469:             equity=p4_input(c["equity_alloc"]),
470:             total_cash=p4_input(c["cash_alloc"]),
471:             nonshort_restriction=p4_input(c["P4_nonshort"]),
472:         )
473:         for sid, a in post["assets"].items():
474:             maps["prices"][sid].update(price=p4_input(a["mark"]), lot=p4_input(a["lot"]))
475:             maps["status"][sid].update(tradable=a["tradable"], in_universe=a["in_universe"])
476:             maps["exposures"][sid].update(industry=a["industry"], beta=p4_input(a["beta"]))
477:             maps["permissions"][sid].update(
478:                 short_permission=a["short_permission"],
479:                 borrow_limit_total=p4_input(a["borrow_limit"]),
480:                 sellable_qty=p4_input(sellable(before["assets"][sid], post["session"])),
481:             )
482:         if c["equity_alloc"] <= 0 or c["cash_alloc"] < 0:
483:             return {"passed": False, "reason": "INFEASIBLE_P4_BUDGET_PROJECTION"}
484:         return p4_assess(
485:             x,
486:             maps,
487:             {k: a["quantity"] for k, a in before["assets"].items()},
488:             {k: a["quantity"] for k, a in post["assets"].items()},
489:             post=True,
490:         )
491: 
492:     def _match(self, d):
493:         before = self.store.state
494:         order = before["orders"][d["order_id"]]
495:         r = d["slice"]
496:         if order["status"] not in LIVE:
497:             self._observe("MATCH_TERMINAL_ORDER_REFUSED", d)
498:             return
499:         for decision in self.decisions.values():
500:             if (
501:                 decision.get("request") is not None
502:                 and decision["request"].raw.decode("utf-8") == order["P4_request_utf8"]
503:             ):
504:                 verify_portfolio_result(
505:                     decision["request"], decision["result"], self.root / "portfolio-runs"
506:                 )
507:         if r["price"] is None or not r["tradable"]:
508:             self._observe("MATCH_NOT_TRADABLE", d)
509:             return
510:         liquidity = visible_adv(self.config, order["security_id"], self.current_key[0])
511:         if not liquidity["passed"]:
512:             self._observe("MATCH_SKIPPED_LIQUIDITY", liquidity)
513:             return
514:         actual = execute_price(order, money(r["price"]))
515:         if actual is None:
516:             self._observe("MATCH_SKIPPED_PRICE_DOMAIN", d)
517:             return
518:         n = fill_quantity(
519:             order, before["capacity"][r["slice_id"]], before["assets"][r["security_id"]]["quantity"]
520:         )
521:         if not n:
522:             self._observe("MATCH_NO_CAPACITY_LOT", d)
523:             return
524:         signed = n if order["side"] == "BUY" else -n
525:         fee = commission(n, actual)
526:         fill_id = self.uid("fill:" + order["order_id"] + ":" + r["slice_id"])
527:         post = deepcopy(before)
528:         o = post["orders"][order["order_id"]]
529:         old_remaining = o["remaining"]
530:         o["remaining"] -= n
531:         o["signed_remaining"] -= signed
532:         o["filled_qty"] += n
533:         o["reserve_notional"] *= o["remaining"] / old_remaining
534:         transition(o, "FILLED" if o["remaining"] == 0 else "PARTIALLY_FILLED")
535:         b = post["budgets"][o["budget_id"]]
536:         b["paid"] += fee
537:         b["turnover_notional"] += n * actual
538:         fill = trade(
539:             post,
540:             fill_id=fill_id,
541:             sid=o["security_id"],
542:             signed_qty=signed,
543:             p=actual,
544:             fee=fee,
545:             session=post["session"],
546:             at=timestamp(self.current_key[0]),
547:             raw_price=money(r["price"]),
548:         )
549:         post["capacity"][r["slice_id"]] -= n
550:         financial = assess(
551:             post,
552:             o["constraints"],
553:             sid=o["security_id"],
554:             signed_qty=signed,
555:             before=before,
556:             turnover=b["turnover_notional"] / b["decision_equity"],
557:         )
558:         conservative = self._p4_post(post, before, o)
559:         if not financial["passed"] or not conservative["passed"]:
560: 
561:             def reject(s):
562:                 cancel(s["orders"][order["order_id"]], "CANCELLED", "RISK_RECHECK_FAILED")
563:                 return [
564:                     {
565:                         "kind": "RiskRejected",
566:                         "local_stage": 70,
567:                         "actual": financial,
568:                         "P4_budget": conservative,
569:                     }
570:                 ]
571: 
572:             self._commit("MATCH_RISK_CANCELLED", d, reject)
573:             return
574: 
575:         def apply(s):
576:             s.clear()
577:             s.update(post)
578:             return [
579:                 {"kind": "Fill", "local_stage": 80, "fill": fill},
580:                 {
581:                     "kind": "AccountUpdated",
582:                     "local_stage": 90,
583:                     "account": account(s),
584:                     "actual_risk": financial,
585:                     "P4_budget_risk": conservative,
586:                 },
587:             ]
588: 
589:         self._commit("MATCH", d, apply, key=fill_id)
590: 
591:     def _cancel(self, kind, d):
592:         if kind == "CANCELLATION" and utc(d["available_at"]) < utc(d["received_at"]):
593:             raise ContractError("STALE_CANCELLATION_TIME")
594: 
595:         def apply(s):
596:             if kind == "CANCELLATION":
597:                 if d["order_id"] not in s["orders"]:
598:                     raise ContractError("UNKNOWN_CANCELLATION_ORDER")
599:                 orders = [s["orders"][d["order_id"]]]
600:             else:
601:                 orders = [o for o in s["orders"].values() if o["expires_session"] <= d["session"]]
602:             changed = []
603:             for o in orders:
604:                 if o["status"] in LIVE:
605:                     cancel(o, "CANCELLED" if kind == "CANCELLATION" else "EXPIRED", kind)
606:                     changed.append(o["order_id"])
607:             return [{"kind": kind, "local_stage": 65, "order_ids": changed, "control": d}]
608: 
609:         self._commit(kind, d, apply)
610: 
611:     def _borrow(self, d):
612:         def apply(s):
613:             total = F(0)
614:             for sid in s["assets"]:
615:                 total += borrow(s, self.uid(f"borrow:{d['session']}:{sid}"), sid)
616:             return [{"kind": "Borrow", "local_stage": 95, "fee": total}]
617: 
618:         self._commit("BORROW", d, apply)
619:         if account(self.store.state)["available"] < 0:
620:             raise ContractError("BORROW_OR_MARGIN_BREACH")
621: 
622:     def _valuation(self, d):
623:         for a in self.store.state["assets"].values():
624:             if a["quantity"] and (not a["mark"] or d["session"] - a["last_mark_session"] > 1):
625:                 raise ContractError("STALE_POSITION_MARK")
626: 
627:         def apply(s):
628:             s["close_quantities"] = {sid: a["quantity"] for sid, a in s["assets"].items()}
629:             s["valuations"].append(
630:                 {
631:                     "kind": "SESSION_CLOSE",
632:                     "session": d["session"],
633:                     "at": timestamp(self.current_key[0]),
634:                     **readonly_snapshot(s),
635:                 }
636:             )
637:             return [{"kind": "Valuation", "local_stage": 100, "account": account(s)}]
638: 
639:         self._commit("VALUATION", d, apply)
640: 
641:     def _flow(self, d):
642:         def apply(s):
643:             before = account(s)["equity"]
644:             amount = money(d["amount"])
645:             s["cash_set"] += amount
646:             s["external_flows"] += amount
647:             _post(
648:                 s, d["flow_id"], "EXTERNAL_FLOW", {"cash_set": amount, "external_capital": -amount}
649:             )
650:             s["valuations"].append(
651:                 {
652:                     "kind": "EXTERNAL_FLOW",
653:                     "at": timestamp(self.current_key[0]),
654:                     "before_equity": before,
655:                     "amount": amount,
656:                     **readonly_snapshot(s),
657:                 }
658:             )
659:             return [{"kind": "ExternalFlow", "local_stage": 10}]
660: 
661:         self._commit("CASHFLOW", d, apply, key=d["flow_id"])
662:         if account(self.store.state)["available"] < 0:
663:             raise ContractError("EXTERNAL_WITHDRAWAL_EXCEEDS_AVAILABLE")
664: 
665:     def _action(self, kind, d):
666:         sid = d["security_id"]
667: 
668:         def apply(s):
669:             if kind == "SPLIT":
670:                 split(s, d["action_id"], sid, money(d["value"]))
671:             elif kind == "DIVIDEND":
672:                 q = (
673:                     s["close_quantities"].get(sid, F(0))
674:                     if d["unit"] == "PER_OLD_SHARE"
675:                     else s["assets"][sid]["quantity"]
676:                 )
677:                 accrue(
678:                     s,
679:                     d["action_id"],
680:                     sid,
681:                     q * money(d["value"]),
682:                     kind="DIVIDEND_EX",
683:                     pay_at=d["pay_at"],
684:                 )
685:             elif kind == "DIVIDEND_PAY":
686:                 pay(s, d["action_id"], self.uid("pay:" + d["action_id"]))
687:             elif kind == "DELIST":
688:                 for o in s["orders"].values():
689:                     if o["security_id"] == sid and o["status"] in LIVE:
690:                         cancel(o, "CANCELLED", "DELIST_LIQUIDATION")
691:                 liquidate(
692:                     s,
693:                     event_id=d["action_id"],
694:                     sid=sid,
695:                     recovery_price=money(d["value"]),
696:                     session=s["session"],
697:                     at=timestamp(self.current_key[0]),
698:                 )
699:             else:
700:                 raise ContractError("UNKNOWN_SIMULATION_EVENT")
701:             return [{"kind": kind, "local_stage": 10, "action_id": d["action_id"]}]
702: 
703:         self._commit(kind, d, apply)
704:         if account(self.store.state)["available"] < 0:
705:             raise ContractError("ACTION_OR_MARGIN_BREACH")

18: def guard_sources(request):
19:     """Reject link/alias substitutions, supplementing the original manifest/hash gate."""
20:     import json
21:     from pathlib import Path
22: 
23:     x = load(request.raw)
24:     for b in x["source"].get("research_bindings", []):
25:         root = guard_path(Path(b["ledger_root"]) / b["run_id"])
26:         manifest = json.loads(guard_path(root / "manifest.json").read_bytes())
27:         for e in manifest["files"]:
28:             guard_path(root / e["path"])
29:         source = b["snapshot"]
30:         root = guard_path(Path(source["root"]) / source["snapshot_id"])
31:         manifest = json.loads(guard_path(root / "manifest.json").read_bytes())
32:         for e in manifest["tables"].values():
33:             guard_path(root / e["path"])

36: def verify_volume_history(config):
37:     """Actual research sources bind every volume revision to original P1 table bytes."""
38:     from pathlib import Path
39: 
40:     from aqrp.core.contracts import SnapshotRef
41:     from aqrp.data.snapshot import verify_snapshot
42: 
43:     sources = {}

54:     dates = {s["session_id"] for s in config["sessions"]}

57:             r["security_id"],
58:             r["session_id"],
59:             r["revision_id"],
60:             None if r["shares"] is None else F(r["shares"]),
61:             utc(r["event_time"]),
62:             utc(r["available_at"]),
63:         )
64:         for r in config["sidecar"]["volume_history"]
65:     }

72:                 r["revision_id"],
73:                 None if r["volume"] is None else F(r["volume"]),

79:             and str(r["session_date"]) in dates
80:             and r["calendar_id"] == calendar
81:         }
82:         if actual != expected:

86: class SignalProvider:
87:     def __init__(self, decisions):
88:         self._requests = {}
89:         for d in decisions:
90:             raw = d["portfolio_request_utf8"].encode("utf-8")
91:             request = PortfolioRequest(raw)
92:             guard_sources(request)
93:             x, _, _, _ = prepare(request)
94:             t = utc(x["context"]["decision_time"])
95:             if t in self._requests:
96:                 raise ContractError("DUPLICATE_DECISION_TIME")
97:             self._requests[t] = raw
98: 
99:     def release(self, now):
100:         raw = self._requests.get(utc(now))
101:         if raw is None:
102:             raise ContractError("SIGNAL_NOT_AVAILABLE_AT_DECISION")
103:         request = PortfolioRequest(raw)
104:         guard_sources(request)
105:         prepare(request)  # Re-read terminal P3 and P1 original bytes at actual release.
106:         return request

109: def adapt_portfolio(request, state):
110:     x, _, _, _ = prepare(request)
111:     x = deepcopy(x)
112:     decision = utc(x["context"]["decision_time"])
113:     c = account(state)
114:     if c["cash_alloc"] < 0 or c["equity_alloc"] <= 0:
115:         raise ContractError("INFEASIBLE_P4_BUDGET_PROJECTION")
116:     if any(o["status"] in ("ACCEPTED", "PARTIALLY_FILLED") for o in state["orders"].values()):
117:         raise ContractError("REBALANCE_PENDING_ORDERS")
118:     for name in ("prices", "status", "exposures", "permissions"):
119:         e = x["inputs"][name]
120:         times = []
121:         for row in e["data"]:
122:             a = state["assets"][row["security_id"]]
123:             if a["mark_ref"] is None or utc(a["mark_ref"]["available_at"]) > decision:
124:                 raise ContractError("NO_VISIBLE_DECISION_MARK")
125:             r = a["mark_ref"]
126:             times.append(r["available_at"])
127:             row.update(
128:                 event_time=r["event_time"],
129:                 available_at=r["available_at"],
130:                 as_of=x["context"]["decision_time"],
131:             )
132:             if name == "prices":
133:                 row.update(price=p4_input(a["mark"]), lot=p4_input(a["lot"]))
134:             elif name == "status":
135:                 row.update(tradable=a["tradable"], in_universe=a["in_universe"])
136:             elif name == "exposures":
137:                 row.update(industry=a["industry"], beta=p4_input(a["beta"]))
138:             else:
139:                 row.update(
140:                     short_permission=a["short_permission"],
141:                     borrow_limit_total=p4_input(a["borrow_limit"]),
142:                     sellable_qty=p4_input(sellable(a, state["session"])),
143:                 )
144:         e.update(
145:             event_time=max(times, key=utc),
146:             available_at=max(times, key=utc),
147:             as_of=x["context"]["decision_time"],
148:         )
149:     e = x["inputs"]["account"]
150:     e.update(
151:         event_time=x["context"]["decision_time"],
152:         available_at=x["context"]["decision_time"],
153:         as_of=x["context"]["decision_time"],
154:     )
155:     e["data"].update(
156:         equity=p4_input(c["equity_alloc"]),
157:         total_cash=p4_input(c["cash_alloc"]),
158:         restricted_cash=p4_input(c["short_value"] + c["P4_nonshort"]),
159:         short_restriction=p4_input(c["short_value"]),
160:         nonshort_restriction=p4_input(c["P4_nonshort"]),
161:         positions=[
162:             {"security_id": k, "quantity": p4_input(a["quantity"])}
163:             for k, a in sorted(state["assets"].items())
164:         ],
165:         valuation_time=x["context"]["decision_time"],
166:         price_ref=x["inputs"]["prices"]["ref"],
167:     )
168:     x["profile"]["fee_reserve"] = "0"
169:     entries = [v for k, v in x["inputs"].items() if k != "scores"] + x["inputs"]["scores"]
170:     for e in entries:
171:         e["content_sha256"] = digest({k: v for k, v in e.items() if k != "content_sha256"})
172:     x["source"]["fixture_manifest"] = {e["ref"]: e["content_sha256"] for e in entries}
173:     out = PortfolioRequest(canonical(x))
174:     prepare(out)
175:     return out, c

178: def signal_identity(request):
179:     x = load(request.raw)
180:     return [
181:         {
182:             "factor_ref": e["data"]["factor_ref"],
183:             "params_hash": e["data"]["params_hash"],

191: def verify_portfolio_result(request, returned, root):
192:     import hashlib
193: 
194:     guard_sources(request)
195:     ledger = ExperimentLedger(guard_path(root))
196:     run = returned["run_id"]
197:     manifest = ledger.verify(run)
198: 
199:     def read(name):
200:         rows = [e for e in manifest["files"] if e["path"] == name]
201:         if len(rows) != 1:
202:             raise ContractError("P4_ARTIFACT_NOT_TERMINAL_MANIFESTED")
203:         path = guard_path(ledger.path(run) / name)
204:         raw = path.read_bytes()
205:         if path.is_symlink() or hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != rows[0]["sha256"]:
206:             raise ContractError("P4_TERMINAL_BYTES_CHANGED")
207:         return raw
208: 
209:     original = read("result.json")
210:     if (
211:         canonical({k: v for k, v in returned.items() if k != "manifest"}) != original
212:         or returned["manifest"] != manifest
213:     ):
214:         raise ContractError("P4_RETURN_TERMINAL_BINDING_MISMATCH")
215:     if returned["status"] == "SUCCEEDED":
216:         candidate = build_candidate(request)
217:         if candidate.raw != read("candidate.json") or load(read("risk.json")) != returned["risk"]:
218:             raise ContractError("P4_CANDIDATE_RISK_BYTES_CHANGED")
219:         audit = audit_published(original, request, candidate)
220:         if load(read("published-byte-audit.json")) != audit:
221:             raise ContractError("P4_PUBLISHED_AUDIT_CHANGED")

18: def construct_engineering(request, ledger_root, code_commit):
19:     policy = reviewed_policy()
20:     if not isinstance(request, PortfolioRequest):
21:         raise ContractError("FROZEN_REQUEST_REQUIRED")
22:     ledger = ExperimentLedger(ledger_root)
23:     config = {
24:         "schema_version": "portfolio-run/1.0.0",
25:         "raw_request_hex": request.raw.hex(),
26:         "request_sha256": request.content_hash,
27:     }
28:     environment = runtime_manifest()
29:     verify_runtime(environment)
30:     lineage = {
31:         "code_commit": code_commit,
32:         "code_bundle": policy["bundle_sha256"],
33:         "data_hash": request.content_hash,
34:         "environment": environment,
35:         "environment_hash": digest(environment),
36:         "config_hash": digest(config),
37:         "code_commit_binding": "Caller-declared; clean SDK separately binds source tree and wheel bytes",
38:         "protocol_hash": digest(
39:             {"profile": "SYNTHETIC_PORTFOLIO_1", "G1": "265af16353d85ce8c2d229a7517809d707498da8"}
40:         ),
41:         "permission_domain": "ENGINEERING_TEST_ONLY",
42:         "seed": 0,
43:     }
44:     run_id = ledger.start(config, lineage)
45:     result = {
46:         "schema_version": "portfolio-result/1.0.0",
47:         "run_id": run_id,
48:         "scope": "ENGINEERING_TEST_ONLY",
49:         "financial_approval": "NONE",
50:         "executable": False,
51:         "request_hash": request.content_hash,
52:         "code_bundle": policy["bundle_sha256"],
53:         "input_lineage": lineage,
54:         "status": "REJECTED",
55:     }
56:     try:
57:         candidate = build_candidate(request)
58:         candidate_data = load(candidate.raw)
59:         validated_input = load(request.raw)
60:         result["context"] = validated_input["context"]
61:         result["selection"] = candidate_data["selection"]
62:         result["rounding"] = {
63:             k: candidate_data[k]
64:             for k in ("ideal", "rounded", "rounding_residual", "unchanged_holdings")
65:         }
66:         decision = check_target(candidate, request)
67:         ledger.artifact(run_id, "candidate.json", load(candidate.raw))
68:         ledger.artifact(run_id, "risk.json", decision)
69:         result["risk"] = decision
70:         if decision["passed"]:
71:             target = {
72:                 **decision["post"]["metrics"],
73:                 "scope": "ENGINEERING_TEST_ONLY",
74:                 "executable": False,
75:                 "request_hash": request.content_hash,
76:                 "risk_hash": decision["content_sha256"],
77:                 "context": validated_input["context"],
78:             }
79:             target["content_sha256"] = digest(target)
80:             result["target"] = target
81:             result["rebalance_proposals"] = [
82:                 {
83:                     "security_id": sid,
84:                     "delta_qty": qty,
85:                     "executable": False,
86:                     "scope": "ENGINEERING_TEST_ONLY",
87:                     "source_price_ref": load(request.raw)["inputs"]["prices"]["ref"],
88:                     "source_account_ref": validated_input["inputs"]["account"]["ref"],
89:                     "decision_time": load(request.raw)["context"]["decision_time"],
90:                     "request_hash": request.content_hash,
91:                 }
92:                 for sid, qty in sorted(target["deltas"].items())
93:                 if qty != "0"
94:             ]
95:             result["status"] = "SUCCEEDED"
96:             audit_published(canonical(result), request, candidate)
97:         else:
98:             result["reasons"] = sorted(
99:                 {
100:                     c["code"]
101:                     for phase in ("pre", "post")
102:                     for c in decision[phase]["checks"]
103:                     if not c["passed"]
104:                 }
105:             )
106:         ledger.artifact(run_id, "result.json", result)
107:         if result["status"] == "SUCCEEDED":
108:             persisted = (ledger.path(run_id) / "result.json").read_bytes()
109:             ledger.artifact(
110:                 run_id, "published-byte-audit.json", audit_published(persisted, request, candidate)
111:             )
112:         ledger.finish(run_id, "SUCCEEDED" if result["status"] == "SUCCEEDED" else "FAILED")
113:     except ContractError as exc:
114:         if (ledger.path(run_id) / "result.json").exists():
115:             ledger.artifact(
116:                 run_id, "publication-failure.json", {"code": exc.code, "not_published": True}
117:             )
118:             ledger.finish(run_id, "FAILED")
119:             raise
120:         result["status"] = "INFEASIBLE" if exc.code.startswith("INFEASIBLE") else "REJECTED"
121:         result.pop("target", None)
122:         result.pop("rebalance_proposals", None)
123:         result["reasons"] = [exc.code]
124:         result["error"] = {
125:             "code": exc.code,
126:             "field": exc.field,
127:             "request_id": run_id,
128:             "risk": "NOT_EVALUATED_INVALID_INPUT",
129:         }
130:         ledger.artifact(run_id, "result.json", result)
131:         ledger.finish(run_id, "FAILED")
132:     except Exception as exc:
133:         ledger.artifact(run_id, "failure.json", {"type": type(exc).__name__, "message": str(exc)})
134:         ledger.finish(run_id, "FAILED")
135:         raise
136:     result["manifest"] = ledger.verify(run_id)
137:     wire = canonical(result)
138:     decoded = load(wire)
139:     decoded.pop("manifest")
140:     if canonical(decoded) != (ledger.path(run_id) / "result.json").read_bytes():
141:         raise ContractError("PUBLISHED_RETURN_BYTES_CHANGED")
142:     return PortfolioResult(wire)
143: 
144: 
145: def construct_approved(request, ledger_root, code_commit):
146:     """Real financial approval is deferred; package policy cannot grant it from caller flags."""
147:     policy = reviewed_policy()
148:     if policy["financial_approval"] == "NONE":
149:         # Do not run any ranking/target arithmetic or fall back to the engineering path.
150:         ledger = ExperimentLedger(ledger_root)
151:         raw = (
152:             request.raw
153:             if isinstance(request, PortfolioRequest)
154:             else canonical({"invalid_input_type": type(request).__name__})
155:         )
156:         lineage = {
157:             "code_commit": code_commit,
158:             "code_bundle": policy["bundle_sha256"],
159:             "data_hash": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
160:             "environment": {"python": sys.version},
161:             "protocol_hash": digest({"formal": "NOT_APPROVED"}),
162:             "permission_domain": "FORMAL_APPROVAL_GATE",
163:             "seed": 0,
164:         }
165:         run_id = ledger.start({"raw_request_hex": raw.hex()}, lineage)
166:         result = {
167:             "schema_version": "portfolio-result/1.0.0",
168:             "run_id": run_id,
169:             "status": "REJECTED",
170:             "reasons": ["APPROVAL_REQUIRED"],
171:             "scope": "FORMAL_APPROVAL_GATE",
172:             "financial_approval": "NONE",
173:             "executable": False,
174:         }
175:         ledger.artifact(run_id, "result.json", result)
176:         result["manifest"] = ledger.finish(run_id, "FAILED")
177:         return PortfolioResult(canonical(result))
178:     raise ContractError("UNAPPROVED_EXECUTION_ENVIRONMENT")
179: 
180: 
181: def replay_engineering(ledger_root, run_id):
182:     """Verify immutable original result and hashes; no financial reapproval or new order."""
183:     ledger = ExperimentLedger(ledger_root)
184:     manifest = ledger.verify(run_id)
185:     if any(e["path"] == "publication-failure.json" for e in manifest["files"]):
186:         raise ContractError("PUBLICATION_FAILED_NOT_REPLAYABLE")
187:     rows = [e for e in manifest["files"] if e["path"] == "result.json"]
188:     if len(rows) != 1:
189:         raise ContractError("RESULT_NOT_IN_TERMINAL_MANIFEST")
190:     path = ledger.path(run_id) / "result.json"
191:     raw = path.read_bytes()
192:     if path.is_symlink() or hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != rows[0]["sha256"]:
193:         raise ContractError("RESULT_BYTES_CHANGED")
194:     result = json.loads(raw)
195:     return {
196:         "status": "IMMUTABLE_ORIGINAL_RESULT_VERIFIED",
197:         "result": result,
198:         "manifest": manifest,
199:         "financial_approval": "NONE",
200:     }

===== src/aqrp/experiments/ledger.py sha256=b71ea44491c5435252d18a66744faee62c29dce530018a4dd5edf3ee71e3786d =====
1: """Durable append-only hash chains, UUID runs and whole-config artifact integrity."""
2: 
3: import hashlib
4: import json
5: import os
6: import re
7: import time
8: import uuid
9: from contextlib import contextmanager
10: from datetime import UTC, datetime
11: from pathlib import Path
12: 
13: import duckdb
14: 
15: from aqrp.core.canonical import canonical, digest
16: from aqrp.core.errors import ContractError
17: from aqrp.data.snapshot import safe_path
18: 
19: IS_WINDOWS = os.name == "nt"
20: 
21: 
22: @contextmanager
23: def mutex(path, timeout=5):
24:     f = Path(path).open("a+b")
25:     if f.tell() == 0:
26:         f.write(b"0")
27:         f.flush()
28:     start = time.monotonic()
29:     while True:
30:         try:
31:             f.seek(0)
32:             if IS_WINDOWS:
33:                 import msvcrt
34: 
35:                 msvcrt.locking(f.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
36:             else:
37:                 import fcntl
38: 
39:                 fcntl.flock(f.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
40:             break
41:         except OSError as exc:
42:             if time.monotonic() - start >= timeout:
43:                 f.close()
44:                 raise ContractError("LEDGER_LOCK_TIMEOUT") from exc
45:             time.sleep(0.01)
46:     try:
47:         yield
48:     finally:
49:         f.seek(0)
50:         if IS_WINDOWS:
51:             import msvcrt
52: 
53:             msvcrt.locking(f.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
54:         else:
55:             import fcntl
56: 
57:             fcntl.flock(f.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
58:         f.close()
59: 
60: 
61: def read_chain(path):
62:     if not Path(path).exists():
63:         return []
64:     previous = "0" * 64
65:     records = []
66:     for index, line in enumerate(Path(path).read_bytes().splitlines()):
67:         item = json.loads(line)
68:         value = {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k != "hash"}
69:         if value["seq"] != index or value["previous"] != previous or digest(value) != item["hash"]:
70:             raise ContractError("LEDGER_CHAIN_CORRUPTED")
71:         previous = item["hash"]
72:         records.append(item)
73:     return records
74: 
75: 
76: def append_chain(path, kind, payload):
77:     records = read_chain(path)
78:     item = {
79:         "seq": len(records),
80:         "recorded_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="microseconds"),
81:         "previous": records[-1]["hash"] if records else "0" * 64,
82:         "kind": kind,
83:         "payload": payload,
84:     }
85:     item["hash"] = digest(item)
86:     with Path(path).open("ab") as f:
87:         f.write(canonical(item) + b"\n")
88:         f.flush()
89:         os.fsync(f.fileno())
90:     return item
91: 
92: 
93: def immutable_write(path, content):
94:     with Path(path).open("xb") as f:
95:         f.write(content)
96:         f.flush()
97:         os.fsync(f.fileno())
98: 
99: 
100: class ExperimentLedger:
101:     def __init__(self, root):
102:         self.root = Path(root)
103:         self.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
104: 
105:     def path(self, run_id):
106:         try:
107:             key = str(uuid.UUID(run_id))
108:         except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError) as exc:
109:             raise ContractError("INVALID_RUN_ID") from exc
110:         return self.root / key
111: 
112:     def start(self, config, lineage):
113:         required = {
114:             "code_commit",
115:             "code_bundle",
116:             "data_hash",
117:             "environment",
118:             "protocol_hash",
119:             "permission_domain",
120:             "seed",
121:         }
122:         if not required <= set(lineage) or not re.fullmatch("[0-9a-f]{40}", lineage["code_commit"]):
123:             raise ContractError("INCOMPLETE_RUN_LINEAGE")
124:         content = canonical(config)
125:         metadata = canonical(lineage)
126:         run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
127:         root = self.path(run_id)
128:         root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
129:         immutable_write(root / "config.json", content)
130:         immutable_write(root / "lineage.json", metadata)
131:         append_chain(
132:             root / "events.jsonl",
133:             "RUNNING",
134:             {"config_hash": digest(config), "lineage_hash": digest(lineage)},
135:         )
136:         return run_id
137: 
138:     def event(self, run_id, kind, payload):
139:         root = self.path(run_id)
140:         with mutex(root / ".lock"):
141:             events = read_chain(root / "events.jsonl")
142:             if not events or events[-1]["kind"] in ("SUCCEEDED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED"):
143:                 raise ContractError("IMMUTABLE_TERMINAL_RUN")
144:             return append_chain(root / "events.jsonl", kind, payload)
145: 
146:     def artifact(self, run_id, name, payload):
147:         if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9_-]+\.(json|html)", name) or name in (
148:             "config.json",
149:             "lineage.json",
150:             "manifest.json",
151:         ):
152:             raise ContractError("INVALID_RUN_ARTIFACT")
153:         root = self.path(run_id)
154:         with mutex(root / ".lock"):
155:             if read_chain(root / "events.jsonl")[-1]["kind"] in (
156:                 "SUCCEEDED",
157:                 "FAILED",
158:                 "CANCELLED",
159:             ):
160:                 raise ContractError("IMMUTABLE_TERMINAL_RUN")
161:             immutable_write(
162:                 root / name,
163:                 payload.encode("utf-8") if name.endswith(".html") else canonical(payload),
164:             )
165: 
166:     def finish(self, run_id, status):
167:         if status not in ("SUCCEEDED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED"):
168:             raise ContractError("INVALID_TERMINAL_STATUS")
169:         root = self.path(run_id)
170:         with mutex(root / ".lock"):
171:             events = read_chain(root / "events.jsonl")
172:             if events[-1]["kind"] in ("SUCCEEDED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED"):
173:                 raise ContractError("IMMUTABLE_TERMINAL_RUN")
174:             manifest = {
175:                 "run_id": run_id,
176:                 "status": status,
177:                 "event_prefix_hash": events[-1]["hash"],
178:                 "files": [
179:                     {"path": p.name, "sha256": hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
180:                     for p in sorted(root.iterdir())
181:                     if p.suffix in (".json", ".html")
182:                 ],
183:             }
184:             immutable_write(root / "manifest.json", canonical(manifest))
185:             append_chain(
186:                 root / "events.jsonl",
187:                 status,
188:                 {
189:                     "manifest_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
190:                         (root / "manifest.json").read_bytes()
191:                     ).hexdigest()
192:                 },
193:             )
194:         return self.verify(run_id)
195: 
196:     def verify(self, run_id):
197:         root = self.path(run_id)
198:         events = read_chain(root / "events.jsonl")
199:         if not events or events[-1]["kind"] not in ("SUCCEEDED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED"):
200:             raise ContractError("NONTERMINAL_RUN")
201:         raw = (root / "manifest.json").read_bytes()
202:         m = json.loads(raw)
203:         if (
204:             hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != events[-1]["payload"]["manifest_sha256"]
205:             or m["event_prefix_hash"] != events[-2]["hash"]
206:             or m["status"] != events[-1]["kind"]
207:             or m["run_id"] != run_id
208:         ):
209:             raise ContractError("RUN_MANIFEST_MISMATCH")
210:         for entry in m["files"]:
211:             p = safe_path(root, entry["path"])
212:             if (root / entry["path"]).is_symlink() or hashlib.sha256(
213:                 p.read_bytes()
214:             ).hexdigest() != entry["sha256"]:
215:                 raise ContractError("RUN_ARTIFACT_HASH_MISMATCH")
216:         return m
217: 
218:     def decision(self, run_id, record):
219:         """Post-run decisions never mutate terminal research artifacts."""
220:         manifest = self.verify(run_id)
221:         with mutex(self.root / ".decision-lock"):
222:             return append_chain(
223:                 self.root / "decisions.jsonl",
224:                 "HUMAN_RESEARCH_DECISION",
225:                 {"run_id": run_id, "manifest_hash": digest(manifest), "record": record},
226:             )
227: 
228:     def register_trial(self, run_id, family, hypothesis, *, budget, hypothesis_spec=None):
229:         if not family or not hypothesis or type(budget) is not int or budget < 1:
230:             raise ContractError("INVALID_EXPERIMENT_FAMILY")
231:         root = self.path(run_id)
232:         config = json.loads((root / "config.json").read_bytes())
233:         lineage = json.loads((root / "lineage.json").read_bytes())
234:         fingerprint_config = {k: v for k, v in config.items() if k not in ("family", "candidates")}
235:         if "candidates" in config:
236:             fingerprint_config["candidates"] = [
237:                 {k: v for k, v in c.items() if k != "hypothesis"} for c in config["candidates"]
238:             ]
239:         fingerprint = digest(
240:             {
241:                 "config": fingerprint_config,
242:                 "lineage": {
243:                     k: v for k, v in lineage.items() if k not in ("protocol_hash", "code_commit")
244:                 },
245:                 "hypothesis_spec": {
246:                     k: v
247:                     for k, v in (hypothesis_spec or {}).items()
248:                     if k not in ("hypothesis", "score_key")
249:                 },
250:             }
251:         )
252:         with mutex(self.root / ".trials-lock"):
253:             past = read_chain(self.root / "trials.jsonl")
254:             peers = [x for x in past if x["payload"]["family"] == family]
255:             repeated = [
256:                 x["payload"]["run_id"] for x in past if x["payload"]["fingerprint"] == fingerprint
257:             ]
258:             item = {
259:                 "run_id": run_id,
260:                 "family": family,
261:                 "hypothesis": hypothesis,
262:                 "fingerprint": fingerprint,
263:                 "repeat_of": repeated,
264:                 "over_budget": len(peers) >= budget,
265:                 "prior_data_attempts": sum(
266:                     r["payload"].get("data_hash") == lineage["data_hash"] for r in past
267:                 ),
268:                 "data_hash": lineage["data_hash"],
269:                 "first_validation_access_at": min(
270:                     (
271:                         r["recorded_at"]
272:                         for r in past
273:                         if r["payload"].get("data_hash") == lineage["data_hash"]
274:                     ),
275:                     default=read_chain(root / "events.jsonl")[0]["recorded_at"],
276:                 ),
277:             }
278:             append_chain(self.root / "trials.jsonl", "TRIAL_ATTEMPT", item)
279:         self.event(run_id, "TRIAL_REGISTERED", item)
280:         if item["over_budget"]:
281:             raise ContractError("EXPERIMENT_BUDGET_EXCEEDED")
282:         return item
283: 
284:     def cache_get(self, key):
285:         path = self.root / ("cache-" + digest(key) + ".json")
286:         if not path.exists():
287:             return None
288:         item = json.loads(path.read_bytes())
289:         if item["key"] != key or item["checksum"] != digest(item["payload"]):
290:             raise ContractError("CACHE_CORRUPTION")
291:         return item["payload"]
292: 
293:     def cache_put(self, key, payload):
294:         data = canonical({"key": key, "payload": payload, "checksum": digest(payload)})
295:         path = self.root / ("cache-" + digest(key) + ".json")
296:         with mutex(self.root / ".cache-lock"):
297:             if path.exists():
298:                 if path.read_bytes() != data:
299:                     raise ContractError("NONDETERMINISTIC_CACHE_RESULT")
300:             else:
301:                 immutable_write(path, data)
302: 
303:     def catalogue(self):
304:         rows = []
305:         for p in self.root.glob("*/manifest.json"):
306:             m = self.verify(p.parent.name)
307:             rows.append({"run_id": m["run_id"], "status": m["status"]})
308:         if not rows:
309:             return []
310:         import pandas as pd
311: 
312:         with duckdb.connect(":memory:") as db:
313:             db.register("runs", pd.DataFrame(rows))
314:             return db.sql("SELECT * FROM runs ORDER BY run_id").df().to_dict("records")
315: 
316:     def compare(self, a, b):
317:         ma, mb = self.verify(a), self.verify(b)
318:         return {
319:             "a": a,
320:             "b": b,
321:             "status_equal": ma["status"] == mb["status"],
322:             "artifact_differences": sorted(
323:                 set((r["path"], r["sha256"]) for r in ma["files"])
324:                 ^ set((r["path"], r["sha256"]) for r in mb["files"])
325:             ),
326:         }
327: 
328:     def family(self, family, declarations, results=None, *, definitions=None):
329:         if not declarations or len(set(declarations)) != len(declarations):
330:             raise ContractError("INVALID_FAMILY_DECLARATIONS")
331:         with mutex(self.root / ".family-lock"):
332:             path = self.root / ("family-" + digest(family) + ".jsonl")
333:             rows = read_chain(path)
334:             if not rows:
335:                 append_chain(path, "DECLARED", {"ids": declarations, "definitions": definitions})
336:                 rows = read_chain(path)
337:             if rows[0]["payload"] != {"ids": declarations, "definitions": definitions}:
338:                 raise ContractError("FAMILY_REDEFINED_AFTER_REGISTRATION")
339:             if results is not None:
340:                 if not set(results) <= set(declarations):
341:                     raise ContractError("UNREGISTERED_HYPOTHESIS")
342:                 append_chain(path, "RESULTS", results)
343:                 rows = read_chain(path)
344:             latest = {}
345:             duplicates = set()
346:             for row in rows[1:]:
347:                 for k, v in row["payload"].items():
348:                     if k in latest:
349:                         duplicates.add(k)
350:                     latest[k] = v
351:             return {
352:                 "ids": declarations,
353:                 "results": latest,
354:                 "duplicate_hypotheses": sorted(duplicates),
355:             }
